# AI Index — Big Ambitions Modding Reference

This file is an AI-oriented entry point for the **Big Ambitions Modding Reference** repository.

The repository is an unofficial reverse-engineering reference containing decompiled C# source from Big Ambitions builds, together with generated Markdown documentation designed to make the codebase easier to navigate and understand.

The `.cs` files contain the actual decompiled implementation.

The `.md` files form a navigation and reference layer around the source code. They identify types, their relationships to other types, and provide links that can be followed to investigate related parts of the codebase.

---

## How AI Agents Should Use This Repository

When answering a question about Big Ambitions' internal implementation, treat this repository as a connected codebase rather than a collection of independent files.

A recommended investigation process is:

1. Identify a relevant system, concept, or type from the question.
2. Find a relevant `.md` reference in this index.
3. Read the `.md` file.
4. Examine its **Uses** section to find types it depends on.
5. Follow those references when they are relevant.
6. Examine **Used By** when you need to determine what calls or depends on the current type.
7. Open the corresponding `.cs` source when implementation details are required.
8. Continue following relationships across directories, namespaces, and assemblies when necessary.
9. Treat the `.cs` source as the final technical reference.

Do not assume that a directory, namespace, or assembly represents the boundary of a system. Important behavior may cross multiple assemblies.

The generated `.md` relationships are intended primarily as navigation aids. They are not a replacement for reading the actual C# implementation.

### Recommended Investigation Pattern

```text
Question
   ↓
Find relevant type
   ↓
Read Type.md
   ↓
Check Uses
   ↓
Follow relevant references
   ↓
Inspect .cs source
   ↓
Check Used By when necessary
   ↓
Continue across assemblies
   ↓
Trace the complete behavior
```

---

## GitHub Crawler / Directory Browsing Limitation

Some AI systems may have difficulty navigating this repository through GitHub's directory browsing pages.

In particular, some automated systems may be unable to fetch GitHub URLs using the:

```text
github.com/.../tree/main/...
```

format because of crawler or `robots.txt` restrictions.

This is a limitation of the AI system's method of accessing GitHub and is **not a restriction imposed by this repository**.

If directory browsing is unavailable, use the direct file links provided in this index instead.

GitHub file URLs use the:

```text
github.com/.../blob/main/...
```

format.

The direct file links in this index are therefore intentional. They allow an AI to jump directly to a relevant reference without first traversing GitHub's directory structure.

---

## Repository Structure

```text
Big-Ambitions-Modding-Reference/
│
├── README.md
├── AI_INDEX.md
│
└── Builds/
    │
    └── Build 3674/
        │
        ├── [Assembly directories]/
        │   ├── *.cs
        │   └── *.md
        │
        └── ZZZ_RootFiles/
            ├── *.cs
            └── *.md
```

### Builds

Each game build is kept separate so that implementations from different versions of Big Ambitions can be investigated independently.

Do not assume that code from different builds is interchangeable. When answering a build-specific question, prefer references from the requested build.

### Assembly Directories

Assembly directories represent the assemblies from which the C# code was decompiled.

A system may span multiple assemblies. Follow the generated **Uses** and **Used By** relationships across assembly boundaries when investigating behavior.

### ZZZ_RootFiles

`ZZZ_RootFiles` contains files that were originally located at the root of the decompiled build rather than inside one of the main assembly directories.

The directory name intentionally sorts these files toward the bottom of directory listings and keeps the large number of root-level decompiled files organized.

# AI-Assisted Reverse Engineering

This repository can be used to investigate questions about the game's internal implementation.

Examples include:

- How does a particular game system work?
- Which classes are involved in a feature?
- What calls a particular method?
- How do two systems interact?
- How are messages generated and sent?
- How are businesses registered?
- How does employee behavior work?
- Which systems are involved in implementing a particular mod?
- Where should a mod interact with an existing game system?

For these questions, do not stop at the first class that appears relevant.

Use the reference graph to follow the implementation until the behavior is understood.

For example:

```text
Question
   ↓
Relevant Type
   ↓
Type.md
   ↓
Uses / Used By
   ↓
Related Type.md
   ↓
Related .cs
   ↓
Additional Types
   ↓
Complete behavior
```

---

# Understanding the Markdown Files

Generated Markdown files generally contain:

- The type name
- The type category
- Declared types
- A link to the main source file
- **Uses** — types referenced or depended upon by the current type
- **Used By** — types that reference or depend upon the current type
- Notes

The **Uses** and **Used By** sections should be treated as navigation aids for tracing the codebase.

They are generated automatically and may not represent every semantic relationship in the game.

---

# Source of Truth

The decompiled `.cs` files are the primary technical reference.

Markdown files are provided to make those files easier to discover and navigate.

When a generated Markdown relationship, summary, or inference conflicts with the actual C# source, prefer the C# source.

When a question requires understanding the exact implementation of a method, field, constructor, condition, or control flow, inspect the corresponding `.cs` file.

---

# Important Limitations

This repository is based on decompiled game assemblies and is therefore a reverse-engineering reference rather than the original source code.

The generated dependency information is intended for navigation and may not detect every relationship in the code.

Relationships involving reflection, dynamically constructed types, strings, external systems, runtime-generated behavior, or complex language features may not be represented completely.

The absence of a relationship in a `.md` file does not necessarily mean that two systems are unrelated.

Important conclusions should be verified against the actual `.cs` implementation.

---

# Build-Specific Investigation

When a question specifies a particular Big Ambitions build, use references from that build whenever possible.

Different builds may contain:

- Added or removed classes
- Changed method implementations
- Changed fields
- Changed dependencies
- Changed modding functionality
- Changed game behavior

Do not automatically assume that an implementation from one build applies to another.

---

# Repository Purpose

This repository exists to make Big Ambitions' decompiled internal code easier for humans and AI systems to explore, understand, and use as a modding and reverse-engineering reference.

It is an unofficial community reference and is not affiliated with or endorsed by the developers or publisher of Big Ambitions.
